      "multiCorrect": false,

      "solution": "**Identify.** The de-Broglie wavelength of a neutron in thermal equilibrium with heavy water at a temperature $ T $ (Kelvin) and mass $ m $, is :\n\n**Concept.** Use the matching wave/optics relation (resonance length, fringe, lens formula) before numbers.\n\n**Work.** Write the governing fact/formula for “Physics General” in one line, substitute carefully, and score every option against it.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) $ \\frac { h } { \\sqrt { \\mathrm { mkT } } } $\n✓ (2) $ \\frac { \\mathrm { h } } { \\sqrt { 3 \\mathrm { mkT } } } $\n✗ (3) $ \\frac { 2 \\mathrm {~h} } { \\sqrt { 3 \\mathrm { mkT } } } $\n✗ (4) $ \\frac { 2 h } { \\sqrt { \\mathrm { mkT } } } $\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(2) $ \\frac { \\mathrm { h } } { \\sqrt { 3 \\mathrm { mkT } } } $**.
